    √ voids the cert in the same transaction as the status write (20 ms)
    √ a non-reversal transition does NOT open a tx or void a cert (10 ms)
    √ force AUDIT_PASSED -> REJECTED voids the live cert (leaving cert-live, off-track) (11 ms)
    √ force CERTIFIED -> REJECTED voids the live cert (10 ms)
    √ forward AUDIT_PASSED -> APPROVED does NOT void (stays on the cert track) (12 ms)
    √ forward APPROVED -> CERTIFIED does NOT void (stays on the cert track) (11 ms)
    √ A1+A2 combined — reverted passed app: cert voided AND cannot be force-resurrected (19 ms)
  H — reviewer/force transition writes the audit row IN the tx (onAudit hook)
    √ DOCUMENT_REVIEWER DOC_APPROVED runs in the tx and wires statusTransitionAuditHook({tx}) (10 ms)
    √ ADMIN force -> AUDIT_PASSED also writes the audit row in the tx (force path) (17 ms)

    √ GRANT+REVOKE precedence: same permission granted AND revoked → REVOKE wins (absent) (1 ms)
    √ unknown/invalid stored permission in a GRANT → ignored (not in effective, no throw) (1 ms)
    √ deterministic sort — effective is sorted ascending (1 ms)
    √ fail-safe: prisma.findMany throws → falls back to role-only + logs, does not throw (1 ms)
    √ unknown role → empty role baseline (no throw) (1 ms)
  userHasEffectivePermission
    √ true when role holds the permission (no grants) (1 ms)
    √ false when role lacks it and no GRANT (1 ms)
    √ true after a GRANT of a role-lacked permission (1 ms)
    √ false after a REVOKE of a role-held permission (1 ms)

    √ maps an unresolved P2002 to a 409 (not a 500) (3 ms)
    √ re-raises a non-P2002 error unchanged (2 ms)
